# Seed 52 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small (Iteration 310)

`seed 52 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier --cpus 2,8`,
commit 8680d8694 (Iteration 301) - the first run with the after-seed-51 batch: alignhot;
> and 0= as opcodes. Started 14:10 UTC Oct 15; the evolution 14:15-17:34 (3 h 19 min, the
owner's 6 hours less the comparison's), the comparison packed by 19:51. 15,246 designs
measured (15,152 alive), 4,440 screened.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest +1.8%, smallest +0.00%, memory
+0 KB, hypervolume +0.5%, best Q -1.9% - **small**. The Q-best design of all runs: seed
51's 04edc97281, 0.148 at 28,125 bytes, 168 KB (Q 0.212 of s6's).

From the run's database (lab/evolve/seed-facts.py) and lab/evolve/heldout.py:

- **Deaths: 92 refused before running - and 2 on the corpus**, the first in these runs:
  b21c6f6763 and 171214ba65, both JIT designs. Reproduced here; the JIT off, both live;
  reverting any one of folds, ops10, rtloop or tag2 makes each live - a JIT fault needing
  all of them, the output ending in the corpus's DO LOOP +LOOP I J UNLOOP LEAVE EXIT tests
  (HANDOFF.md, open item 0a).
- The batch's genes: alignhot on none of the front; > on 3 (as a word, and in the fused
  >?BRANCH), 0= on none; 2@ on 77 (52 in seed 51); opinline and knumber on the front.
- Children: mutation 56.6%, crossover 31.9%, borrowed 11.4%. The front, 164 designs:
  mutation 110, carried 26, borrowed 20, crossover 8. The JIT on 89.
- Held out (heldout.py):
  sieve (held out): 164 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.937
    the 10 fastest by selection, their rank on it: 5 4 8 9 10 1 2 3 6 7
    held-out / selection speed: min 0.37, median 0.66, max 2.57
    flattered most by the selection: 38d2979006 2.57, 757adddab3 2.49, 1facb9dd51 2.47, 01b47b02b3 2.42, 6ee46ea622 2.40

---

# Evolved VM designs

15246 designs evaluated, 15152 alive, 4440 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 00c4d388bf, speed 0.138, binary + image 28,245, memory 168 KB - Q 0.200 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
